# Logistics

_Shopee Open Platform API v2_

---

## v2.logistics.get_shipping_parameter

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_parameter`

Use this api to get the parameter "info_needed" from the response to check if the order has pickup or dropoff or no integrate options. This api will also return the addresses and pickup time id options for the pickup method. For dropoff, it can return branch id, sender real name etc, depending on the 3PL requirements. [Please call this API when order status is READY_TO_SHIP or package logistics/fulfillment status is LOGISTICS_READY].

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_parameter` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_parameter` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_parameter` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_parameter` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_sn | string | ✅ | 201214JASXYXY6 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| package_number | string |  | OFG134731496217591 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is't a package number. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 2880a5a28510424eaa3288fd941fae2c | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ info_needed | object |  |  | The parameters required based on each specific order to Init. Must use the fields included under info_needed to call Init. For logistic_id 80003 and 80004, both Regular and JOB shipping methods are supported. If you choose Regular shipping method, please use "tracking_no" to call Init API. If you choose JOB shipping method, please use "sender_real_name" to call Init API. Note that only one of "tracking_no" and "sender_real_name" can be selected. |
| ↳ ↳ dropoff | string[] |  | [ ] | Could contain 'branch_id', 'sender_real_name' or 'tracking_no'. If it contains 'branch_id', choose one to Init. If it contains 'sender_real_name' or 'tracking_no', should manually input these values in Init API. If it has empty value, developer should still include "dropoff" field in Init API.Could contain 'slug'.If it contains 'slug', to return selected 3PL partner only for TW C2C sellers to drop-off parcels with. |
| ↳ ↳ pickup | string[] |  | ["address_id", "pickup_time_id"] | Could contain 'address_id' and 'pickup_time_id'. Choose one address_id and its corresponding pickup_time_id to Init. If it has empty value, developer should still include "pickup" field in Init API.It could contains "tracking_number" returned from "info_need"for some channels, please also add it when init. |
| ↳ ↳ non_integrated | string[] |  |  | Could contain 'tracking_no'. If it contains 'tracking_no', should manually input these values in Init API. If it has empty value, developer should still include "non-integrated" field in Init API. |
| ↳ dropoff | object |  |  | Logistics information for dropoff mode order. |
| ↳ ↳ branch_list | object[] |  |  | List of available dropoff branches info. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ branch_id | int |  |  | The identity of logistics branch. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ region | string |  | SG | The region of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ state | string |  |  | The state of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ city | string |  | Asajaya | The city of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address | string |  |  | The address description of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ zipcode | string |  |  | The zipcode of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ district | string |  | Sarawak | The district of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ town | string |  |  | The town of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ slug_list | object[] |  |  |  List of available TW 3PL drop-off partners. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ slug | string |  |  |  The identity of slug. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ slug_name | string |  |  |  The name of slug. |
| ↳ pickup | object |  |  | Logistics information for pickup mode order. |
| ↳ ↳ address_list | object[] |  |  | List of available pickup address info. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address_id | int64 |  | 234 | The identity of address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ region | string |  | SG | The region of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ state | string |  | Sarawak | The state of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ city | string |  |  | The city of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ district | string |  |  | The district of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ town | string |  | haha | The town of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address | string |  |  | The address description of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ zipcode | string |  | 50003 | The zipcode of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address_flag | string[] |  |  | The flag of shop address, applicable values: default_address, pickup_address, return_address, current_address(only for multi-warehouse sellers) |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ time_slot_list | object[] |  |  | List of pickup_time information corresponding to the address_id.Some logistics channels may not return any date or time for pickup time slots. In such cases, sellers can arrange shipment without selecting any time slot, and Shopee will arrange a suitable timing for these situations. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ date | timestamp |  | 1608103685 | The date of pickup time. In timestamp. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ time_text | string |  |  | The text description of pickup time. Only applicable for certain channels. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ pickup_time_id | string |  |  | The identity of pickuptime. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ flags | string[] |  | ["recommended"] | This field will have the value “recommended” for the time slot that Shopee suggests sellers choose.&nbsp;While it is advisable for sellers to choose the recommended time slot, they can also choose other time slots that do not have the recommended flag. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_parameter?access_token=access_token&order_sn=201214JASXYXY6&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.request("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
curl --location --request GET 'https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_parameter?shop_id=shop_id&partner_id=partner_id&order_sn=201214JASXYXY6&access_token=access_token&timestamp=timestamp&sign=sign' 
```

### Response Example

```json
{
    "error": "",
    "message": "",

        "info_needed": {
            "dropoff": [],
            "pickup": [
                "address_id",
                "pickup_time_id"
            ]
        },
        "dropoff": null,
        "pickup": {
            "address_list": [
                {
                    "address_id": 123,
                    "region": "SG",
                    "state": "",

                    "district": "",
                    "town": "",

                        "default_address",

                },
                {

                    "address": "hhh, #34",

    },

}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "error_not_found",
    "message": "Wrong parameters, detail: the order is not found.",
    "request_id": "44c2f2077941a5393c402cc42daf246e"
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `logistics.error_param` | The order is being allocated, please wait until the allocate is completed. |
| `logistics.error_channel_exist` | Fail to find the fulfillment channel of this order. |
| `error_not_found` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | System error. Please try again later. |
| `common.invalid_shop` | Shop id is invalid. Please check your shop. |
| `logistics.package_number_not_exist` | Please request with package_number for this split order. |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `error_param` | There is no access_token in query. |
| `error_auth` | Invalid access_token. |
| `error_param` | Invalid partner_id. |
| `error_param` | There is no partner_id in query. |
| `error_auth` | No permission to current api. |
| `error_param` | There is no sign in query. |
| `error_sign` | Wrong sign. |
| `error_param` | no timestamp |
| `error_param` | Invalid timestamp |
| `error_network` | Inner http call failed |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | OFG list type is not TO_PROCESS |
| `error_param` | Shipping parameters can only be obtained when package is ready to be shipped |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `logistics.no_available_time_slot` | No available time slot |
| `logistics.no_supported_dropoff_branch` | No supported drop-off branch |
| `logistics.no_supported_pickup_address` | No supported pickup address |
| `logistics.no_valid_shipping_parameters` | No valid shipping parameters. Please contact support |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |
| `error_status` | The order has been cancelled. |
| `error_param` | Package cannot be rescheduled |
| `error_other` | Rescheduling dependency error |

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Order Management
- Swam ERP

| Date | Details |

 |

## v2.logistics.get_mass_shipping_parameter

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_shipping_parameter`

Use this api to get parameter "info_needed" from response to check if order has pickup or dropoff or no integrate options. For pickup, will also return addresses and pickup time options. For dropoff, will also return branch id, sender real name etc, depending on 3PL requirements. Can only batch request for multiple packages under same product_location_id and logistics_channel_id. [Please call this API when order status is READY_TO_SHIP or package logistics/fulfillment status is LOGISTICS_READY].

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_shipping_parameter` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_shipping_parameter` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_shipping_parameter` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_shipping_parameter` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| logistics_channel_id | int64 |  | 50021 | The API can only batch request the shipping parameter for multiple packages under the same product_location_id and same logistics_channel_id.&nbsp;Use this field to specify the logistics_channel_id for the request. If not specified, will use the logistics_channel_id corresponds to the first package_number by default. |
| product_location_id | string |  | "VN0002BIZ" | The API can only batch request the shipping parameter for multiple packages under the same product_location_id and same logistics_channel_id.&nbsp;Use this field to specify the product_location_id for the request. If not specified, will use the product_location_id corresponds to the first package_number by default. |
| package_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of packages you want to get shipping parameters. limit [1, 50]. |
| ↳ package_number | string | ✅ | ["OFG188728166212046"] | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You shouldn't fill the field with empty string when there isn't a package number. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 2880a5a28510424eaa3288fd941fae2c | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened.&lt;path&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;path&gt;&lt;/path&gt; |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ info_needed | object |  |  | The parameters required based on each specific order to Init. Must use the fields included under info_needed to call Init. For logistic_id 80003 and 80004, both Regular and JOB shipping methods are supported. If you choose Regular shipping method, please use "tracking_no" to call Init API. If you choose JOB shipping method, please use "sender_real_name" to call Init API. Note that only one of "tracking_no" and "sender_real_name" can be selected. |
| ↳ ↳ dropoff | string[] |  | [] | Could contain 'branch_id', 'sender_real_name', or 'tracking_no'. If it contains 'branch_id', choose one to Init. If it contains 'sender_real_name' or 'tracking_no', should manually input these values in Init API. If it has empty value, developer should still include "dropoff" field in Init API. |
| ↳ ↳ pickup | string[] |  | ["address_id", "pickup_time_id"] | Could contain 'address_id' and 'pickup_time_id'. Choose one address_id and its corresponding pickup_time_id to Init. If it has empty value, developer should still include "pickup" field in Init API. It could contains "tracking_number" returned from "info_need"for some channels, please also add it when init. |
| ↳ ↳ non_integrated | string[] |  |  | Could contain 'tracking_no'. If it contains 'tracking_no', should manually input these values in Init API. If it has empty value, developer should still include "non-integrated" field in Init API. |
| ↳ dropoff | object |  |  | Logistics information for dropoff mode package. |
| ↳ ↳ branch_list | object[] |  |  | List of available dropoff branches info. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ branch_id | int64 |  |  | The identity of logistics branch. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ region | string |  |  | The region of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ state | string |  |  | The state of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ city | string |  |  | The city of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address | string |  |  | The address description of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ zipcode | string |  |  | The zipcode of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ district | string |  |  | The district of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ town | string |  |  | The town of specify address. |
| ↳ pickup | object |  |  | Logistics information for pickup mode package. |
| ↳ ↳ address_list | object[] |  |  | List of available pickup address info. For Multi-Warehouse sellers, note that changing pickup address from Current may incur higher shipping fees. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address_id | int64 |  |  | The identity of address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ region | string |  |  | The region of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ state | string |  |  | The state of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ city | string |  |  | The city of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ district | string |  |  | The district of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ town | string |  |  | The town of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address | string |  |  | The address description of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ zipcode | string |  |  | The zipcode of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address_flag | string[] |  |  | The flag of shop address, applicable values: default_address, pickup_address, return_address, current_address (Multi-Warehouse sellers only) |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ time_slot_list | object[] |  |  | List of pickup_time information corresponding to the address_id.Some logistics channels may not return any date or time for pickup time slots. In such cases, sellers can arrange shipment without selecting any time slot, and Shopee will arrange a suitable timing for these situations. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ date | timestamp |  |  | The date of pickup time. In timestamp. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ time_text | string |  |  | The text description of pickup time. Only applicable for certain channels. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ pickup_time_id | string |  |  | The identity of pickuptime. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ flags | string[] |  | ["recommended"] | This field will have the value “recommended” for the time slot that Shopee suggests sellers choose.&nbsp;While it is advisable for sellers to choose the recommended time slot, they can also choose other time slots that do not have the recommended flag. |
| ↳ success_list | object[] |  |  | Success package list. |
| ↳ ↳ package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order.&nbsp; |
| ↳ fail_list | object[] |  |  | Fail package list. |
| ↳ ↳ package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order.&nbsp; |
| ↳ ↳ fail_reason | string |  |  | Reason for failure. |

```python
}
```

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Order Management
- Swam ERP

### Update Log

| 2025-02-24 | New API |


---

## v2.logistics.ship_order

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/ship_order`

Use this api to initiate logistics including arrange pickup, dropoff or shipment for non-integrated logistic channels. Should call v2.logistics.get_shipping_parameter to fetch all required param first. It's recommended to initiate logistics one hour after the orders were placed.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/ship_order` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/ship_order` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/ship_order` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/ship_order` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_sn | string | ✅ | 201212DCXHJUIKJ | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is't a package number. |
| pickup | object |  |  | Required parameter ONLY if get_shipping_parameter returns "pickup" under "info_needed". Developer should still include "pickup" field in the call even if "pickup" has empty value.	 |
| ↳ address_id | int64 | ✅ |  | The identity of address. Retrieved from v2.logistics.get_shipping_parameter. |
| ↳ pickup_time_id | string |  |  | The pickup time id. Retrieved from v2.logistics.get_shipping_parameter, you can only select one from the time_slot_list.&nbsp;Some logistics channels may not return any date or time for pickup time slots. In such cases, sellers can arrange shipment without selecting any time slot, and Shopee will arrange a suitable timing for these situations. |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  |  | Need input this field when "tracking_number" is returned from "info_need". Please note that this tracking number is assigned by third-party shipping carrier for item shipment.	 |
| dropoff | object |  |  | Required parameter ONLY if get_shipping_parameter returns "dropoff" under "info_needed". Developer should still include "dropoff" field in the call even if "dropoff" has empty value. For logistic_id 80003 and 80004, both Regular and JOB shipping methods are supported. If you choose Regular shipping method, please use "tracking_no" to call Init API. If you choose JOB shipping method, please use "sender_real_name" to call Init API. Note that only one of "tracking_no" and "sender_real_name" can be selected. |
| ↳ branch_id | int64 |  | 0 | The identity of branch.  |
| ↳ sender_real_name | string |  |  | The real name of sender.	 |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  |  | Need input this field when "tracking_number" is returned from "info_need". Please note that this tracking number is assigned by third-party shipping carrier for item shipment.	 |
| ↳ slug | string |  |  |  The selected 3PL partner to drop-off parcels with. |
| non_integrated | object |  |  | Optional parameter when get_shipping_parameter returns "non-integrated" under "info_needed".	 |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  |  | Optional parameter for non-integrated channel order. The tracking number assigned by the shipping carrier for item shipment.	 |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 3dad66f43b8447d282ae6da36626c6b7 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/ship_order?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "order_sn": "201212DCXHJUIKJ",
  "package_number": "",
  "pickup": {
    "address_id": 0,
    "pickup_time_id": "",
    "tracking_number": ""
  }
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.request("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"order_sn": "201212DCXHJUIKJ",
	"package_number": "",
	"pickup": {
		"address_id": 0,
		"pickup_time_id": "",
		"tracking_number": ""
	}
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
    "error": "",
    "message": "",
    "request_id": "3dad66f43b8447d282ae6da36626c6b7"
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "logistics.ship_order_need_sender_real_name",
    "message": "Parameter sender_real_name can not be null.",
    "request_id": "6ea20540431c35f2a76cfbaa0c090683"
}
```

## v2.logistics.mass_ship_order

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/mass_ship_order`

Use this api to initiate logistics including arrange pickup, dropoff or shipment for non-integrated logistic channels. Should call v2.logistics.get_mass_shipping_parameter to fetch all required params first. It's recommended to initiate logistics one hour after the orders were placed since there is one-hour window buyer can cancel any order without request to seller. The API can only batch arrange shipment for multiple packages under the same product_location_id and same logistics_channel_id.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/mass_ship_order` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/mass_ship_order` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/mass_ship_order` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/mass_ship_order` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| logistics_channel_id | int64 |  | 50021 | The API can only batch arrange shipment for multiple packages under the same product_location_id and same logistics_channel_id.&nbsp;Use this field to specify the logistics_channel_id for the request.&nbsp;If not specified, will use the logistics_channel_id corresponds to the first package_number by default. |
| product_location_id | string |  |  | The API can only batch arrange shipment for multiple packages under the same product_location_id and same logistics_channel_id.&nbsp;Use this field to specify the product_location_id for the request.&nbsp;If not specified, will use the product_location_id corresponds to the first package_number by default. |
| package_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of packages you want to arrange shipment. limit [1, 50]. |
| ↳ package_number | string | ✅ |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is't a package number. |
| pickup | object |  |  | Required parameter ONLY if GetParameterForInit returns "pickup" or if GetLogisticsInfo returns "pickup" under "info_needed" for the same order. Developer should still include "pickup" field in the call even if "pickup" has empty value. |
| ↳ address_id | int64 |  |  | The identity of address. |
| ↳ pickup_time_id | string |  |  | The pickup time id.&nbsp;Retrieved from v2.logistics.get_mass_shipping_parameter, you can only select one from the time_slot_list.Some logistics channels may not return any date or time for pickup time slots. In such cases, sellers can arrange shipment without selecting any time slot, and Shopee will arrange a suitable timing for these situations. |
| dropoff | object |  |  | Required parameter ONLY if GetParameterForInit returns "dropoff" or if GetLogisticsInfo returns "dropoff" under "info_needed" for the same order. Developer should still include "dropoff" field in the call even if "dropoff" has empty value. For logistic_id 80003 and 80004, both Regular and JOB shipping methods are supported. If you choose Regular shipping method, please use "tracking_no" to call Init API. If you choose JOB shipping method, please use "sender_real_name" to call Init API. Note that only one of "tracking_no" and "sender_real_name" can be selected. |
| ↳ branch_id | int64 |  | 0 | The identity of branch. |
| ↳ sender_real_name | string |  |  | The real name of sender.	 |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  |  | Need input this field when "tracking_number" is returned from "info_need". Please note that this tracking number is assigned by third-party shipping carrier for item shipment. |
| non_integrated | object |  |  | Optional parameter when get_mass_shipping_parameter returns "non-integrated" under "info_needed". |
| ↳ tracking_number | object[] |  |  | Optional parameter for non-integrated channel order. The tracking number assigned by the shipping carrier for item shipment. |
| ↳ ↳ package_number | string | ✅ |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| ↳ ↳ tracking_number | string | ✅ |  | Optional parameter for non-integrated channel order. The tracking number assigned by the shipping carrier for item shipment. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 3dad66f43b8447d282ae6da36626c6b7 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| success_list | object[] |  |  | Success package list. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| fail_list | object[] |  |  | Fail package list. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| ↳ fail_reason | string |  |  | Reason for failure. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
{
    "package_list": [
        {
            "package_number": "OFG192433821205164"
        },
        {
            "package_number": "OFG192433773204150"
        }
    ],
    "pickup": {
        "address_id": 200000015,
        "pickup_time_id": "1737104400_5"
    }
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
    "error": "",
    "message": "",
    "request_id": "e3e3e7f32deed04cc72ddcb0da0c5301:010002a07d0bfcf4:0000007fae5e5a35",
    "response": {
        "fail_list": [
            {
                "fail_reason": "Package is not under the specified fulfilment channel",
                "package_number": "OFG192433773204150"
            }
        ],
        "success_list": [
            {
                "package_number": "OFG192433821205164"
            }
        ]
    }
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "error_param",
    "message": "Wrong parameters, detail: package_list is a required field.",
    "request_id": "e3e3e7f32bceabc0cc3cc08991674701:0100020420340e60:00000084d7cc7a2c"
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `logistics.error_param` | Duplicate package number |
| `error_param` | Package is not under the specified fulfilment channel |
| `logistics.error_param` | Package is not under the specified warehouse |
| `error_param` | Shop id: {shop_id} didn't set {return_store_name} return store, please go back to Shopee Seller Center to complete the setting. |
| `error_param` | Shop id: {shop_id},return store:{return_store_name} not found. |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| ` logistics.package_already_shipped` | This package has already been arranged shipment and you can't call this API again. |
| `logistics.error_param` | Package exceeds limit. |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |
| `logistics.warehouse_stock_insufficient` | Failed to assign a warehouse with sufficient stock |

- Seller In House System

## v2.logistics.update_shipping_order

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/update_shipping_order`

For pickup method only, use this api to update pickup address and pickup time for orders in "RETRY_SHIP" status.


### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_shipping_order` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/update_shipping_order` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/update_shipping_order` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_shipping_order` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_sn | string | ✅ | 201214JASXYXY6 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is't a package number. |
| pickup | object | ✅ |  | Required parameter ONLY if GetParameterForInit returns "pickup" or if GetLogisticsInfo returns "pickup" under "info_needed" for the same order. Developer should still include "pickup" field in the call even if "pickup" has empty value. |
| ↳ address_id | int64 | ✅ | 126194 | The identity of address. Retrieved from get_shipping_parameter. |
| ↳ pickup_time_id | string | ✅ | 439291 | The pickup time id. Retrieved from get_shipping_parameter. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 3dad66f43b8447d282ae6da36626c6b7 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_shipping_order?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "order_sn": "201214JASXYXY6",
  "package_number": "-",
  "pickup": {
    "address_id": 126194,
    "pickup_time_id": "439291"
  }
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.request("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"order_sn": "201214JASXYXY6",
	"package_number": "-",
	"pickup": {
		"address_id": 126194,
		"pickup_time_id": "439291"
	}
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
    "error": "",
    "message": "",
    "request_id": "3dad66f43b8447d282ae6da36626c6b7"
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "logistics.update_shipping_order_no_param_change",
    "message": "You can’t update shipping with all the same parameters.",
    "request_id": "b3cb59c28de7e9dca43309fbe20f3a6b"
}
```

## v2.logistics.get_tracking_number

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_number`

After arranging shipment (v2.logistics.ship_order) for the integrated channel, use this api to get the tracking_number, which is a required parameter for creating shipping labels. The api response can return tracking_number empty, since this info is dependent from the 3PL, due to this it is allowed to keep calling the api within 5 minutes interval, until the tracking_number is returned.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_number` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_number` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_number` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_number` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_sn | string | ✅ | 201214JASXYXY6 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there isn't a package number. |
| response_optional_fields | string |  | first_mile_tracking_number | Indicate response fields you want to get. Please select from the below response parameters. If you input an object field, all the params under it will be included automatically in the response. If there are multiple response fields you want to get, you need to use English comma to connect them. Available values: plp_number, first_mile_tracking_number,last_mile_tracking_number |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 9d07076ffda5407bb7c559f0b82ed91e | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  | MY200448706479IT | The tracking number of this order. |
| ↳ plp_number | string |  |  | The unique identifier for package of BR correios. |
| ↳ first_mile_tracking_number | string |  | CNF877146678717210312 | The first mile tracking number of the order. Only for Cross Border Seller |
| ↳ last_mile_tracking_number | string |  | 200448706479IT | The last mile tracking number of the order. Only for Cross Border BR seller. |
| ↳ hint | string |  |  Buyers CVS closed， waiting for buyer to reselect another CVS stores，auto cancel time 2021-01-01 | Indicate hint information if cannot get some fields under special scenarios. For example, cannot get tracking_number when cvs store is closed. |
| ↳ pickup_code | string |  |  | For drivers to quickly identify parcel to be picked up.&nbsp;Only returned for ID local orders who using instant+sameday for delivery. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_number?access_token=access_token&order_sn=201214JASXYXY6&package_number=-&partner_id=partner_id&response_optional_fields=first_mile_tracking_number&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.request("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
curl --location --request GET 'https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_number?package_number=-&partner_id=partner_id&order_sn=201214JASXYXY6&access_token=access_token&timestamp=timestamp&sign=sign&response_optional_fields=first_mile_tracking_number&shop_id=shop_id' 
```

### Response Example

```json
{
    "error": "",
    "message": "",
    "response": {
        "tracking_number": "MY200448706479IT",
        "first_mile_tracking_number": "CNF877146678717210312"
    },
    "request_id": "9d07076ffda5407bb7c559f0b82ed91e"
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "logistics.invalid_error",
    "message": "The order is not exist.",
    "request_id": "2524f1aa00d84e7eba5a4d46d5e04791"
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |

| `error_not_found` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | System error. Please try again later. |
| `logistics.invalid_error` | The order is not exist. |
| `logistics.package_not_exist` | The package is not exist. |
| `common.invalid_shop` | Shop id is invalid. Please check your shop. |
| `logistics.package_number_not_exist` | Please request with package_number for this split order. |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `error_param` | There is no access_token in query. |
| `error_auth` | Invalid access_token. |
| `error_param` | Invalid partner_id. |
| `error_param` | There is no partner_id in query. |
| `error_auth` | No permission to current api. |
| `error_param` | There is no sign in query. |
| `error_sign` | Wrong sign. |
| `error_param` | no timestamp |
| `error_param` | Invalid timestamp |
| `error_network` | Inner http call failed |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |

| `error_param` | request not from gateway |

## v2.logistics.get_mass_tracking_number

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_tracking_number`

After arranging shipment (v2.logistics.mass_ship_order) for the integrated channel, use this api to get the tracking_number, which is a required parameter for creating shipping labels. The api response can return tracking_number empty, since this info is dependent from the 3PL, due to this it is allowed to keep calling the api within 5 minutes interval, until the tracking_number is returned.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_tracking_number` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_tracking_number` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_tracking_number` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_mass_tracking_number` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| package_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of packages you want to get tracking number. limit [1, 50]. |
| ↳ package_number | string | ✅ |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there isn't a package number. |
| response_optional_fields | string |  | first_mile_tracking_number | Indicate response fields you want to get. Please select from the below response parameters. If you input an object field, all the params under it will be included automatically in the response. If there are multiple response fields you want to get, you need to use English comma to connect them. Available values: plp_number, first_mile_tracking_number,last_mile_tracking_number. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |

| ↳ ↳ plp_number | string |  |  | The unique identifier for package of BR correios. |
| ↳ ↳ first_mile_tracking_number | string |  | CNF877146678717210312 | The first mile tracking number of the order. Only for Cross Border Seller |
| ↳ ↳ last_mile_tracking_number | string |  | 200448706479IT | The last mile tracking number of the order. Only for Cross Border BR seller. |
| ↳ ↳ hint | string |  | Buyers CVS closed， waiting for buyer to reselect another CVS stores，auto cancel time 2021-01-01 | Indicate hint information if cannot get some fields under special scenarios. For example, cannot get tracking_number when cvs store is closed. |
| ↳ ↳ pickup_code | string |  |  | For drivers to quickly identify parcel to be picked up.&nbsp;Only returned for instant+sameday orders. |

| ↳ ↳ package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |

| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |

| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of orders you want to get. limit [1,50] |
| ↳ order_sn | string | ✅ | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  | 60489687088750 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is't a package number. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 8412939d0fbd48c9a548fba4b710a7f6 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |

## v2.logistics.create_shipping_document

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document`

Use this api to create shipping document task for each order or package and this API is only available after retrieving the tracking number. 

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of order you want to create shipping document. limit [1, 50] |
| ↳ order_sn | string | ✅ | 201118BCKPJQQ8 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  | 2485710696837122445 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is not a package number. |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  | SPXID02742637123B | The tracking number of order. Required except for the channel allow print before arrange shipment. |
| ↳ shipping_document_type | string |  | NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL | The type of shipping document. Available values: NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL, THERMAL_AIR_WAYBILL, NORMAL_JOB_AIR_WAYBILL, THERMAL_JOB_AIR_WAYBILL,&nbsp;THERMAL_UNPACKAGED_LABEL |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 5551ce8db5314c70a362dfe33544f074 | The identifier of the API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | object[] |  |  | Indicate warning message you should take care. |
| ↳ order_sn | string |  | 201118BCKPJKW0 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  | 12345678 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ result_list | object[] |  |  | The list of the result data. |
| ↳ ↳ order_sn | string |  | 201118BCKPJQQ8 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ ↳ package_number | string |  | 2485710696837122445 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_error | string |  | logistics.order_not_exist | Indicate error type if one element hit error. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_message | string |  | The order_sn 201118BCKPJKW0 you provided is not exist. Please check | Indicate error details if one element hit error. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://open.admin.shopee.io/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "order_list": [
    {
      "order_sn": "201118BCKPJQQ8",
      "package_number": "2485710696837122445",
      "shipping_document_type": "NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL",
      "tracking_number": "SPXID02742637123B"
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"order_list": [
		{
			"order_sn": "201118BCKPJQQ8",
			"package_number": "2485710696837122445",
			"tracking_number": "SPXID02742637123B",
			"shipping_document_type": "NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL"
		}
	]
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
    "error":"",
    "message":"",
    "response":{
        "result_list":[
            {
                "order_sn":"22112899JG5XGU"
            }
        ]
    },
    "warning":null,
    "request_id":"80e9644be9c077bec9f89a762e2ea120"
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error":"common.batch_api_all_failed",
    "message":"Failed, please check result_list for more details.",
    "response":{
        "result_list":[
            {
                "order_sn":"2211289U7C0PUK",
                "fail_error":"logistics.package_can_not_print",
                "fail_message":"The package can not print now."
            }
        ]
    },
    "request_id":"3c8a0a3bcd9d767e929aca2fafe2a620"
}
```

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |

| `logistics.package_can_not_print` | The package can not print now.  |
| `logistics.package_number_not_found` | The package_number {package_number} is not exist. |
| `logistics.package_number_not_exist` | Please request with package_number for this split order. |
| `logistics.tracking_number_invalid` | The tracking number is invalid. Please check the tracking number. |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `logistics.can_not_print_combine_order	` | This order is part of a combined parcel, please use Seller Center instead.			 |
| `logistics.can_not_print_jit_order` | This shipping channel only supports document printing in Shopee seller center |

| `error_param` | Invalid partner_id. |

| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_config` | Something is wrong, please try again later. |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |

## v2.logistics.get_shipping_document_result

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_result`

Use this api to retrieve the status of the shipping document task. Document will be available for download only after the status change to 'READY'.



### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_result` |

| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_result` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| ↳ order_sn | string | ✅ | 201118BCKPJQQ8 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  | 2485710696837122445 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is't a package number. |
| ↳ shipping_document_type | string |  | NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL | The type of shipping document. Available values: NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL, THERMAL_AIR_WAYBILL, NORMAL_JOB_AIR_WAYBILL, THERMAL_JOB_AIR_WAYBILL, THERMAL_UNPACKAGED_LABEL |


### Response Parameters

| ↳ result_list | object[] |  |  | The result data list of the API response. |
| ↳ ↳ order_sn | string |  | 201118BCKPJQQ8 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ ↳ package_number | string |  | 2485710696837122445 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| ↳ ↳ status | string |  | READY | The status of the shipping document task you querying with order_sn. Available values: READY， FAILED， PROCESSING |
| ↳ ↳ fail_error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if one element hit error. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if one element hit error. |

## v2.logistics.download_shipping_document

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/download_shipping_document`

Use this api to download shipping_document. You have to call v2.logistics.create_shipping_document to create a new shipping document task first and call v2.logistics.get_shipping_document_resut to get the task status second. If the task is READY, you can download this shipping document.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/download_shipping_document` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/download_shipping_document` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/download_shipping_document` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/download_shipping_document` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| shipping_document_type | string |  | NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL | The type of shipping document. Available values: NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL, THERMAL_AIR_WAYBILL, NORMAL_JOB_AIR_WAYBILL, THERMAL_JOB_AIR_WAYBILL, THERMAL_UNPACKAGED_LABEL |
| order_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of orders you need to download it's shipping document. |
| ↳ order_sn | string | ✅ | 201118BCKPJQQ8 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  | 2485710696837122445 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is't a package number. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| waybill | file |  |  | The waybill file. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://open.admin.shopee.io/api/v2/logistics/download_shipping_document?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "order_list": [
    {
      "order_sn": "201118BCKPJQQ8",
      "package_number": "2485710696837122445"
    }
  ],
  "shipping_document_type": "NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL"
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"shipping_document_type": "NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL",
	"order_list": [
		{
			"order_sn": "201118BCKPJQQ8",
			"package_number": "2485710696837122445"
		}
	]
}
```

### Response Example

```json
If Success, this api will return a waybill file.
If Fail, this api will return the error message as the example below.
{
    "error":"logistics.xxxxx",
    "message":"The package can not print now, please .......",
    "request_id":"814468c0f07157681b78f6f0b58030db"
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "logistics.shipping_document_should_print_first",
    "message": "The package can not print now, please create shipping document first.Detail: these orders: 220725D58XURJS should print",
    "request_id": "774ffe1565034014799a7e221246d614"
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `logistics.error_network` | System error. Please try again later. |
| `error_not_found` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: {msg}. |

| `logistics.unknown_error` | Unknown error, please contact Shopee to check. Detail: {msg} |
| `logistics.error_booking_order` | This order is matched order with booking, don't need arrange shipment through seller side. |
| `logistics.order_not_exist` | The order_sn {order_sn} is not exist. |
| `logistics.package_number_not_found` | The package_number {package_number} is not exist. |
| `logistics.package_print_failed` | Some package failed to print, please try again later. Detail: {msg} |
| `logistics.packages_can_not_download_together` | Packages can not download together. Detail: {msg} |
| `logistics.download_later` | Processing, please download later. Detail: {msg} |

| `error_param` | There is no access_token in query. |

| `error_param` | Invalid partner_id. |
| `error_param` | There is no partner_id in query. |
| `error_auth` | No permission to current api. |
| `error_param` | There is no sign in query. |
| `error_sign` | Wrong sign. |
| `error_param` | no timestamp |
| `error_param` | Invalid timestamp |
| `error_network` | Inner http call failed |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_config` | Something is wrong, please try again later. |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Order Management
- Swam ERP

### Update Log

| Date | Details |
| --- | --- |
| 2025-07-07 | Add THERMAL_UNPACKAGED_LABEL option for shipping_document_type |
| 2021-07-19 | update request example |


---

## v2.logistics.get_shipping_document_data_info

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_data_info`

Use this api to fetch the logistics information of an order, these info can be used for self-design AWB printing. Besides, this api supports returning personal info as images.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_data_info` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_data_info` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_data_info` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_data_info` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_sn | string | ✅ | 201224EM1FMFG1 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| package_number | string |  | 9087129345 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You shouldn't fill the field with empty string when there isn't a package number. |
| recipient_address_info | object[] |  |  | recipient address to query as image |
| ↳ key | string | ✅ | name | fields to query in the recipient address, should be name, phone, full_address,&nbsp;town,&nbsp;district,&nbsp;city,&nbsp;state,&nbsp;region,&nbsp;zipcode. |
| ↳ style | object |  |  | image style |
| ↳ ↳ text_style | string[] |  | ["bold", "italic"] | supports bold and italic |
| ↳ ↳ font_size | int32 |  | 12 | supports 1 - 108 |
| ↳ ↳ text_color | string |  | #39c5bb | color string like "#AbCd12" |
| ↳ ↳ image_width | float |  | 3.0 | supports 0.1-30, in centimeters |
| ↳ ↳ h_align | string |  | center | text horizontal align, supports left, center and right. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 862fc4d77de74edf9bf045c7650f1988 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying.Note: For parcels that support pre-shipment printing, calling this API before shipment will only return the following fields:responserecipient_address_infokeyimageshipping_document_infocodcod_amountorder_weightlogistics_channel_idshipping_carrierpickup_code |
| ↳ recipient_address_info | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ key | string |  | name | queried field in recipient address |
| ↳ ↳ image | string |  | data:image/png;base64,/9j/4AAQSkZJRgABAgEASABIAAD/…… | &nbsp;base64 encoded png data string |
| ↳ shipping_document_info | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ cod | boolean |  | true | This value indicates whether the order was a COD (cash on delivery) order. |
| ↳ ↳ cod_amount | string |  | 185 | Use this field to indicate cod amount. |
| ↳ ↳ order_weight | int32 |  | 500 | Use this field to indicate order weight when calculate the shipping fee. The unit of weigh is gram. |
| ↳ ↳ logistics_channel_id | int32 |  | 28016 | The identity of logistic channel. |

Please ensure the service_code is INCLUDED on your shipping label, otherwise the parcel cannot be processed by the warehouse. If you didn't retrieve service_code after you first called this API, please try few more times within 30 minutes. |

### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_data_info?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "order_sn": "201224EM1FMFG1",
  "package_number": "9087129345",
  "recipient_address_info": [
    {
      "key": "name",
      "style": {
        "font_size": 12,
        "h_align": "center",
        "image_width": 3,
        "text_color": "#39c5bb",
        "text_style": [
          "bold",
          "italic"
        ]
      }
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.request("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "error_not_found",
    "message": "Wrong parameters, detail: the order is not found.",
    "request_id": "f93c90c2b6945787e974dc091a2c78c4"
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `error_status` | Status verification failed, shipping documents of this package cannot be printed now. |
| `logistics.error_param` | The order is being allocated, please wait until the allocate is completed. |
| `error_not_found` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Got an error when trying to generate image, please try again |
| `logistics.error_limit` | You don’t ship order yet. |
| `logistics.error_booking_order` | This order is matched order with booking, don't need arrange shipment through seller side. |
| `logistics.error_param` | You can not get order logistics of warehouse order. |
| `logistics.error_status_limit` | The order status is invalid. Please check the order status. |
| `logistics.package_not_exist` | The package is not exist. |
| `error_channel_unsupport` | This logistic channel/logistic type doesn’t support retrieving info from Open API. Please use v2.logistics.download_shipping_document API instead.			 |
| `error_param` | Not allowed to self design AWB |
| `logistics.package_number_not_exist` | Please request with package_number for this split order. |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `logistics.can_not_print_combine_order	` | This order is part of a combined parcel, please use Seller Center instead.			 |
| `logistics.can_not_print_jit_order` | This shipping channel only supports document printing in Shopee seller center |
| `error_param` | There is no access_token in query. |
| `error_auth` | Invalid access_token. |
| `error_param` | Invalid partner_id. |
| `error_param` | There is no partner_id in query. |
| `error_auth` | No permission to current api. |
| `error_param` | There is no sign in query. |
| `error_sign` | Wrong sign. |
| `error_param` | no timestamp |
| `error_param` | Invalid timestamp |
| `error_network` | Inner http call failed |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |
| `error_param` | This order logistics is not allowed to get. |

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Order Management
- Swam ERP

### Update Log

| Date | Details |
| --- | --- |
| 2025-08-14 | Support for pre-shipment printing of self-designed AWBs for certain logistics channels |
| 2025-07-07 | Add new response fields "sorting_group", "unpackaged_sku_id", "unpackaged_sku_id_qrcode" |
| 2024-04-15 | add new response fields "dely_fri_label", "dely_sat_label", "dely_sun_label"
 |
| 2023-05-12 | add is_mutual_check field for VN/ID shops
 |
| 2023-04-12 | add buyer_prefer_delivery_time only for TW local channel:30017
 |
