## v2.logistics.get_tracking_info

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_info`

Use this api to get the logistics tracking information of an order.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_info` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_info` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_info` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_info` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_sn | string | ✅ | 2409177JCSRTEU | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You shouldn't fill the field with empty string when there is a package number. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | e3e3e7f33c4d75f91fb48ecb41aa2a01:010003d71ed9e184:00000066cec6b739 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ order_sn | string |  | 2409177JCSRTEU | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| ↳ logistics_status | string |  | LOGISTICS_DELIVERY_FAILED | The logistics status for the order. Applicable values: See Data Definition- LogisticsStatus. |
| ↳ tracking_info | object[] |  |  | The tracking info of the order. |
| ↳ ↳ update_time | timestamp |  | 1726561500 | The time when the logistics&nbsp;tracking info is updated. |
| ↳ ↳ description | string |  | 包裹正退回物流中心 | The description of the logistics tracking info. |
| ↳ ↳ logistics_status | string |  | FAILED_DELIVERED | The logistics status for the order. Applicable values: See Data Definition- LogisticsStatus. |
| ↳ reversed_tracking_number | string |  | SG246907296501CU | The tracking number of the reversed logistics.Note: Only apply to the cross-border segment of failed delivery parcels returned from the local return warehouse to the seller. |
| ↳ reversed_courier_name | string |  | SPX Express | The courier name of the reversed logistics.Note: Only apply to the cross-border segment of failed delivery parcels returned from the local return warehouse to the seller. |
| ↳ reversed_tracking_info | object[] |  |  | The tracking information of the reversed logistics.Note: Only apply to the cross-border segment of failed delivery parcels returned from the local return warehouse to the seller. |
| ↳ ↳ update_time | int64 |  | 1729842624 | The time when the reversed logistics&nbsp;tracking info is updated. |
| ↳ ↳ description | string |  | 当地仓库已备货，包裹准备发运 | The description of the reversed logistics tracking info. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_info?access_token=access_token&order_sn=201214JASXYXY6&package_number=9000192023232&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.request("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
curl --location --request GET 'https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_tracking_info?package_number=9000192023232&partner_id=partner_id&order_sn=201214JASXYXY6&access_token=access_token&timestamp=timestamp&sign=sign&shop_id=shop_id' 
```

### Response Example

```json
{
    "error": "",
    "message": "",
    "request_id": "e3e3e7f33c4d75f91fb48ecb41aa2a01:010003d71ed9e184:00000066cec6b739",
    "response": {
        "order_sn": "2409177JCSRTEU",
        "logistics_status": "LOGISTICS_DELIVERY_FAILED",
        "tracking_info": [
            {
                "description": "賣家將於確認訂單後出貨",
                "logistics_status": "ORDER_CREATED",
                "update_time": 1726561320
            },
            {
                "description": "包裹已交接給物流商",
                "logistics_status": "PICKED_UP",
                "update_time": 1726561320
            },
            {
                "description": "包裹正退回物流中心",
                "logistics_status": "FAILED_DELIVERED",
                "update_time": 1726561500
            }
        ],
        "reversed_tracking_number": "SG246907296501CU",
        "reversed_courier_name": "SPX Express",
        "reversed_tracking_info": [
            {
                "description": "当地仓库已备货，包裹准备发运",
                "update_time": 1729842624
            },
            {
                "description": "包裹已出库，正在运送至转运仓",
                "update_time": 1729845466
            }
        ]
    }
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "logistics.invalid_error",
    "message": "The order is not exist.",
    "request_id": "7769bb40266b5cca105b345b0c04e2d4"
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `logistics.error_param` | The order is being allocated, please wait until the allocate is completed. |
| `error_not_found` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | System error. Please try again later. |
| `logistics.invalid_error` | The order is not exist. |
| `logistics.error_status_limit` | The order status is invalid. Please check the order status. |
| `logistics.package_not_exist` | The package is not exist. |
| `common.invalid_shop` | Shop id is invalid. Please check your shop. |
| `logistics.package_number_not_exist` | Please request with package_number for this split order. |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `error_param` | There is no access_token in query. |
| `error_auth` | Invalid access_token. |
| `error_param` | Invalid partner_id. |
| `error_param` | There is no partner_id in query. |
| `error_auth` | No permission to current api. |
| `error_param` | There is no sign in query. |
| `error_sign` | Wrong sign. |
| `error_param` | no timestamp |
| `error_param` | Invalid timestamp |
| `error_network` | Inner http call failed |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |

## v2.logistics.get_address_list

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_address_list`

For integrated logistics channel, use this call to get pickup address for pickup mode order.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_address_list` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_address_list` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_address_list` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_address_list` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 675015f4f59943a39f2f69c22da431f8 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ show_pickup_address | boolean |  | false | Show pickup address or not. |
| ↳ address_list | object[] |  |  | The address list of you shop |
| ↳ ↳ address_id | int64 |  | 1173 | The identity of address. |
| ↳ ↳ region | string |  |  | The region of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ state | string |  |  | The state of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ city | string |  |  | The city of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ address | string |  |  | The address description of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ zipcode | string |  |  | The zipcode of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ district | string |  |  | The district of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ town | string |  |  | The town of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ address_type | string[] |  | ["DEFAULT_ADDRESS","PICK_UP_ADDRESS"] | The flag of shop address.Available values: DEFAULT_ADDRESS, PICK_UP_ADDRESS, RETURN_ADDRESS. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_address_list?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.request("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```json
curl --location --request GET 'https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_address_list?sign=sign&access_token=access_token&timestamp=timestamp&shop_id=shop_id&partner_id=partner_id' 
```

## v2.logistics.update_address

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/update_address`

Use this API to update the address of a shop.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_address` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/update_address` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/update_address` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_address` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| address_id | int64 | ✅ | 123456 | Unique identifier for the address. You can get the address_id via v2.logistics.get_address_list. |
| region | string |  | Brazil | The region of the address.Note: Do not allow to update the region of the address. |
| state | string |  | SP | The state of the address. |
| city | string |  | São Paulo | The city of the address. |
| district | string |  | Pinheiros | The district of the address. |
| town | string |  | Rua dos Pinheiros | The town of the address. |
| address | string |  | 123 Rua dos Pinheiros Apt 45 | The detailed address description of the address. |
| zipcode | string |  | 05422-001 | The zipcode of the address. |
| name | string |  | Carlos Silva | Recipient’s name at this address. |
| phone | string |  | +55-11-91234-5678 | Contact phone number for the recipient. |
| geo_info | string |  |  | Geolocation information for the address.&nbsp;Type: JSON stringNote:&nbsp;1) To clear existing geo info, pass "" or {}.2) To keep existing geo info, do not include this field.3) The JSON may include optional fields:- formattedAddress (string):&nbsp;full formatted address.- region (object) – contains latitude and longitude as floats.- user_verified (boolean) – whether the geolocation is verified by the user.- user_adjusted (boolean) – whether the geolocation was adjusted by the user. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  | NOT_ALLOW_TO_EDIT_ADDRESS_REGION | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | The param is invalid. not allow to edit address region. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| request_id | string |  | 6745b892295d750abf83a29430510400 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_address?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "address": "123 Rua dos Pinheiros Apt 45",
  "address_id": 123456,
  "city": "São Paulo",
  "district": "Pinheiros",
  "geo_info": "-",
  "name": "Carlos Silva",
  "phone": "+55-11-91234-5678",
  "region": "Brazil",
  "state": "SP",
  "town": "Rua dos Pinheiros",
  "zipcode": "05422-001"
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"address_id": 123456,
	"region": "Brazil",
	"state": "SP",
	"city": "São Paulo",
	"district": "Pinheiros",
	"town": "Rua dos Pinheiros",
	"address": "123 Rua dos Pinheiros Apt 45",
	"zipcode": "05422-001",
	"name": "Carlos Silva",
	"phone": "+55-11-91234-5678",
	"geo_info": "-"
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
    "error": "",
    "message": "",
    "request_id": "e3e3e7f344620c52040a7b9217596101:010001e23e83a8eb:000000ca8d26008c"
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "NOT_ALLOW_TO_EDIT_ADDRESS_REGION",
    "message": "The param is invalid. not allow to edit address region.",
    "request_id": "e3e3e7f344620e7f4f65a97017001701:01000129eca6425f:000000b3fea28f31"
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `error_param` | Cannot set inbound pickup address for this address. |
| `error_param` | Inbound pickup address state and city is invalid |
| `error_param` | Inbound pickup address cannot be the same as Shopee warehouse address. |
| `error_param` | Inbound pickup address must be in São Paulo state. |
| `NOT_ALLOW_TO_EDIT_ADDRESS_REGION` | The param is invalid. not allow to edit address region. |

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Order Management

| 2025-11-24 | New API |

## v2.logistics.get_channel_list

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_channel_list`

Use this api to get all supported logistic channels.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_channel_list` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_channel_list` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_channel_list` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_channel_list` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ logistics_channel_list | object[] |  |  | The list of logistics channel. |
| ↳ ↳ logistics_channel_id | int64 |  | 28016 | The identity of logistic channel. |
| ↳ ↳ logistics_channel_name | string |  | VNPost Nhanh_product | The name of logistic channel. |
| ↳ ↳ cod_enabled | boolean |  | true | This is to indicate whether this logistic channel supports COD |
| ↳ ↳ enabled | boolean |  | true | Whether this logistic channel is enabled on shop level. |
| ↳ ↳ fee_type | string |  | SIZE_INPUT | SIZE_SELECTIONSIZE_INPUTFIXED_DEFAULT_PRICECUSTOM_PRICE |
| ↳ ↳ size_list | object[] |  |  | Only for fee_type is SIZE_SELECTION |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ size_id | string |  | 0 | The identity of size. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ name | string |  |  | The name of size. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ default_price | float |  |  | The pre-defined shipping fee for the specific size. |
| ↳ ↳ weight_limit | object |  |  | The weight limit for this logistic channel. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ item_max_weight | float |  | 10.0 | The max weight for an item on this logistic channel.If the value is 0 or null, that means there is no limit. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ item_min_weight | float |  | 0.01 | The min weight for an item on this logistic channel. If the value is 0 or null, that means there is no limit. |
| ↳ ↳ item_max_dimension | object |  |  | The dimension limit for this logistic channel. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ height | float |  | 100.0 | The max height limit. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ width | float |  | 100.0 | The max width limit. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ length | float |  | 100.0 | The max length limit. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ unit | string |  | cm | The unit for the limit. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ dimension_sum | float |  | 280.0 | The sum of the item's dimension |
| ↳ ↳ volume_limit | object |  |  | The limit of item volume. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ item_max_volume | float |  | 10.0 | The max volume for an item on this logistic channel.If the value is 0 or null, that means there is no limit for the item weight. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ item_min_volume | float |  | 1.0 | The min volume for an item on this logistic channel. If the value is 0 or null, that means there is no limit for the item weight. |
| ↳ ↳ logistics_description | string |  |   | For checkout channels, this field indicates its corresponding fulfillment channels. |
| ↳ ↳ force_enable | boolean |  | false | Indicates whether the logistic channel is force enabled on Shop Level. If true, sellers cannot close this channel. |
| ↳ ↳ mask_channel_id | int64 |  | 0 | Indicate the parent logistic channel ID. If it’s 0, it indicates the channel is a checkout(masked) channel; if it’s not 0, indicate the channel is a fulfillment channel and has a checkout channel(checkout channel’s channel_id equals this mask_channel_id) on top of it. Multiple channels may share the same mask_channel_id. |
| ↳ ↳ block_seller_cover_shipping_fee | boolean |  | false | Indicate whether the channel is blocked to use seller cover shipping fee function.if the channel does not allow sellers to cover shipping fee, then the block_seller_cover_shipping_fee field will return true, otherwise it will return false. |
| ↳ ↳ support_cross_border | boolean |  | false | Indicate whether this channel support cross border shipping. |
| ↳ ↳ seller_logistic_has_configuration | boolean |  | false | Indicate If seller has set the Seller logistics configuration if set will return true, otherwise it will return false or null. |
| ↳ ↳ logistics_capability | object |  |  | The capability of one logistic channel. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ seller_logistics | boolean |  | false | Indicate If it's a Seller logistics channel, if it's a Seller logistics channel will return true, otherwise it will return false. |
| ↳ ↳ preprint | boolean |  | false | Indicate whether this channel support pre-print AWB |
| ↳ ↳ service_type_identifier | string |  | instant | This parameter specifies the delivery service type of logistics channel. Applicable values:&nbsp;- instant- same_day- null |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_channel_list?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.request("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

## v2.logistics.update_channel

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/update_channel`

Use this api to update shop level logistics channel's configuration.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_channel` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/update_channel` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/update_channel` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_channel` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| logistics_channel_id | int64 | ✅ | 14623 | The identity of logistic channel. |
| enabled | boolean |  | true | Whether to enable this logistic channel. |
| cod_enabled | boolean |  | true | Whether to enable COD for this logistic channel. Only COD supported channels are applicable. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | df17f35abb4244938b22a675211dc99c | The identifier of the API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ shop_id | int64 |  | 123123 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. |
| ↳ enabled | boolean |  | true | Whether this logistic channel is enabled. |
| ↳ cod_enabled | boolean |  | false | Whether COD is enabled for this channel. |
| ↳ logistics_channel_id | int64 |  | 14623 | The identity of logistic channel. |
| ↳ updated_channels | object[] |  |  | List of channels that are updated in the operation (inclusive of dependent logistics channels) |
| ↳ ↳ channel_id | int64 |  | 80025 | Logistics channel ID |
| ↳ ↳ channel_display_name | string |  | channel Anteraja | Logistics channel name |
| ↳ ↳ unsupport_warehouse | object[] |  |  | List details of unsupported warehouses |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ warehouse_id | int64 |  | 78 | Unsupported warehouse ID |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ warehouse_name | string |  | ID_WHS_03 | Unsupported warehouse name |
| ↳ is_multi_warehouse | boolean |  | false |  |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_channel?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "cod_enabled": True,
  "enabled": True,
  "logistics_channel_id": 14623,
  "preferred": True
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.request("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"logistics_channel_id": 14623,
	"enabled": true,
	"cod_enabled": true
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
	"request_id": "df17f35abb4244938b22a675211dc99c",
	"error": "error_auth",
	"message": "Invalid access_token.",
	"response": {
		"shop_id": 123123,
		"enabled": true,
		"cod_enabled": false,
		"logistics_channel_id": 14623,
                "is_multi_warehouse": true,
		"update_channels": [{
			"channel_id": 1000,
			"channel_display_name": "",
			"unsupport_warehouse": [{
				"warehouse_id": 1,
				"warehouse_name": "abc"
			}]
		}]
	}

}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "logistics.error_param",
    "message": "Invalid logistics_channel_id. Please check the logistics_channel_id.",
    "request_id": "c955f5939580785debf7aaec62ec8d07"
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |

| `logistics_error` | Channel is invalid. Please check the channel id. |
| `error_not_found` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | System error. Please try again later. |
| `logistics.has_item_in_bundle` | channel is not allow to disable due to there is item in the bundle |
| `logistics.error_param` | Invalid logistics_channel_id. Please check the logistics_channel_id. |
| `error` | no supported warehouse address |
| `logistics.minimum_activation_rule` | {parent_channel} cannot be turned off. Please enable at least 1 of the following channel(s): {seller channel name list} |
| `logistics.address_miss_long_lat` | Address miss long lat. |
| `logistics.require_pickup_address` | Please go to set pickup address first. |
| `logistics.not_allow_to_update_channel` | You are not allowed to enable or disable channel. |
| `common.invalid_shop` | Shop id is invalid. Please check your shop. |
| `logistics_error` | Sorry that this masked channel can't be enabled. |
| `logistics_error` | Sorry that this masked channel can't be enabled. |
| `logistics_error` | Sorry that this channel can’t be disabled since it is force enabled. |
| `logistics_error` | Sorry that this channel can't be enabled since it doesn’t pass the validation (whitelist validation / exclusive validation / serviceable validation ). |
| `logistics_error` | Sorry that this masked channel can't be preferred. |
| `logistics_error` | Sorry that this channel can't be enabled(disabled)/preferred/ COD enabled(COD disabled). |
| `error_param` | There is no access_token in query. |
| `error_auth` | Invalid access_token. |
| `error_param` | Invalid partner_id. |
| `error_param` | There is no partner_id in query. |
| `error_auth` | No permission to current api. |
| `error_param` | There is no sign in query. |
| `error_sign` | Wrong sign. |
| `error_param` | no timestamp |
| `error_param` | Invalid timestamp |
| `error_network` | Inner http call failed |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |

## v2.logistics.get_operating_hours

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_operating_hours`

This API is utilized to retrieve the existing operating hours of sellers including Pickup Operating Hours,  Special Hours, Instant Operating Hours, and Shop Collection Operating Hours.

| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_operating_hours` |

| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_operating_hours` |

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: This shop isn't eligible to set up Regular Operating Hours. |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Order Management
- Swam ERP

---

## v2.logistics.get_operating_hour_restrictions

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_operating_hour_restrictions`

This API is designed to retrieve all restrictions related to inputting operating hours for the v2.logistics.update_operating_hours function. This API ensures that user are aware of any limitations or conditions that may affect their operating hours.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_operating_hour_restrictions` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_operating_hour_restrictions` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_operating_hour_restrictions` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_operating_hour_restrictions` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |

| ↳ ↳ working_day_config | object |  |  | The restrictions specific to each day |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ monday | object |  |  | The restrictions specific for Monday |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ mandatory | boolean |  | true | If the value is true, this day must be marked as open. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ minimum_operating_hour | int64 |  | 6 | Minimum number of hours required for the seller to operate on that day. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ minimum_start_time | string |  | 07:00 | The start hour for that day should not be set earlier than this time. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ maximum_start_time | string |  | 10:00 | The start hour for that day should not be set later than this time. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ minimum_end_time | string |  | 16:00 | The end hour for that day should not be set earlier than this time. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ maximum_end_time | string |  | 21:00 | The end hour for that day should not be set later than this time. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ operating_24_hour_toggle | boolean |  | false | If the toggle value is true, the user can set the&nbsp;start_time&nbsp;to&nbsp;00:00&nbsp;and the&nbsp;end_time&nbsp;to&nbsp;23:59&nbsp;to indicate that the shop is operating 24 hours a day. |

### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_operating_hour_restrictions?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.RPCRequest("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: This shop isn't eligible to set up Regular Operating Hours. |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Order Management
- Swam ERP

---

## v2.logistics.update_operating_hours

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/update_operating_hours`

This API is designed to allow sellers to update their operating hours. It is essential that the values provided in this API align with the restrictions retrieved from the v2.logistics.get_operating_hour_restrictions API to ensure compliance with platform requirements. This API uses overwriting updates, when updating pickup operating hours, still need to include all parts even those not needing changes.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_operating_hours` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/update_operating_hours` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/update_operating_hours` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_operating_hours` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| regular_operating_hour | object |  |  | Details of Pickup Operating Hours / Preferred Pickup Hours: You can skip this parameter if you are not updating the Pickup Operating Hours / Preferred Pickup Hours |
| ↳ monday | object |  |  | Operating hours for Monday: You can skip this information if you want to mark the day as closed. |
| ↳ ↳ start_time | string | ✅ | 09:00 | Start time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30. |
| ↳ ↳ end_time | string | ✅ | 18:30 | End time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30.Except for a 24-hour, you can input a start_time of 00:00 and an end_time of 23:59 |
| ↳ tuesday | object |  |  | Operating hours for Tuesday: You can skip this information if you want to mark the day as closed. |
| ↳ ↳ start_time | string | ✅ | 09:00 | Start time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30. |
| ↳ ↳ end_time | string | ✅ | 18:30 | End time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30.Except for a 24-hour, you can input a start_time of 00:00 and an end_time of 23:59 |
| ↳ wednesday | object |  |  | Operating hours for Wednesday: You can skip this information if you want to mark the day as closed. |
| ↳ ↳ start_time | string | ✅ | 09:00 | Start time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30. |
| ↳ ↳ end_time | string | ✅ | 18:30 | End time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30.Except for a 24-hour, you can input a start_time of 00:00 and an end_time of 23:59 |
| ↳ thursday | object |  |  | Operating hours for Thursday: You can skip this information if you want to mark the day as closed. |
| ↳ ↳ start_time | string | ✅ | 09:00 | Start time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30. |
| ↳ ↳ end_time | string | ✅ | 18:30 | End time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30.Except for a 24-hour, you can input a start_time of 00:00 and an end_time of 23:59 |
| ↳ friday | object |  |  | Operating hours for Friday: You can skip this information if you want to mark the day as closed. |
| ↳ ↳ start_time | string | ✅ | 09:00 | Start time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30. |
| ↳ ↳ end_time | string | ✅ | 18:30 | End time for operating hours on that day:The time should be in the format XX:YY, where YY is either 00 or 30.Except for a 24-hour, you can input a start_time of 00:00 and an end_time of 23:59 |

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: This shop isn't eligible to set up Instant Operating Hours. |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: This shop isn't eligible to set up Regular Operating Hours. |
| `error_permission` | Sorry you don't have the permission, detail: This shop isn't eligible to set up Special Operating Hours. |
| `error_param` | All modifications to the operating hours were unsuccessful. Please check the result_list and try again. |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Order Management
- Swam ERP

| Date | Details |

---

## v2.logistics.delete_special_operating_hour

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/delete_special_operating_hour`

This API is used to delete a specific special operating hour for a seller. This API allows sellers to manage their operating hours by removing any special operating hours that are no longer needed. To use this API, the name of the special operating hour to be deleted should be obtained from the v2.logistics.get_operating_hours API.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/delete_special_operating_hour` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/delete_special_operating_hour` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/delete_special_operating_hour` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/delete_special_operating_hour` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | string | ✅ | 3.3 Campaign | Name of the special operating hour which can be retrieved from v2.logistics.get_operating_hours |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  | common.error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| request_id | string |  | 3dad66f43b8447d282ae6da36626c6b7 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/delete_special_operating_hour?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "name": "3.3 Campaign"
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```json
	"name": "3.3 Campaign"
}
```

```json
	"message": "-",
	"error": "-"
}
```

```json
}
```

## v2.logistics.batch_update_tpf_warehouse_tracking_status

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/batch_update_tpf_warehouse_tracking_status`

For CB orders that fulfilled by 3PF, support 3PF Warehouse Vendors to update the tpf_tracking_status when 3PF warehouse receive the order and complete the outbound of the package.
CB orders that fulfilled by 3PF：
v2.shop.get_shop_info  - shop_fulfillment_flag in {Pure - 3PF Shop,PFF - 3PF Shop,LFF Hybrid Shop}
And
v2.order.get_order_detail -  fulfillment_flag = fulfilled_by_local_seller

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/batch_update_tpf_warehouse_tracking_status` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/batch_update_tpf_warehouse_tracking_status` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/batch_update_tpf_warehouse_tracking_status` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/batch_update_tpf_warehouse_tracking_status` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| tpf_name | string | ✅ | Shopee 3PF | The name of 3PF Warehouse Vendor. Prohibit pure numbers and excessive abbreviations. Standardize naming for easy business recognition. Input priority: warehouse English name &gt; full pinyin of warehouse brand name &gt; warehouse Chinese name &gt; other officially recognized and prominent names. |
| tpf_tracking_status | string | ✅ | 3pf_warehouse_outbound_done | The 3PF tracking status for the timestamp. All statuses are in lower case.&nbsp;List of status:&nbsp;- 3pf_warehouse_order_created- 3pf_warehouse_outbound_done |
| package_list | object[] | ✅ |  |  |
| ↳ order_sn | string | ✅ | 2003160SXK2A3T | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  | OFG38027870177402 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| ↳ update_time | int64 | ✅ | 1610000000 | This is to indicate timestamp of the 3PF tracking status.Timestamp should be within order create time and order pick up by 3PL time. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| request_id | string |  | e3e3e7f32b90e1a2b73dc8ca69586501 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ success_list | object[] |  |  | Update success order list. |
| ↳ ↳ order_sn | string |  | 2003160SXK2A3T | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ ↳ package_number | string |  | OFG38027870177402 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| ↳ fail_list | object[] |  |  | Update fail order list. |
| ↳ ↳ order_sn | string |  | 201214JASXYXY6 | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ ↳ package_number | string |  | OFG134731496217591 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_error | string |  | common.error_server | Reason for failure. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_message | string |  | Something wrong. Please try later. | Reason for failure. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/batch_update_tpf_warehouse_tracking_status?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "package_list": [
    {
      "order_sn": "2003160SXK2A3T",
      "package_number": "OFG38027870177402",
      "update_time": 1610000000
    },
    {
      "order_sn": "201214JASXYXY6",
      "package_number": "OFG134731496217591",
      "update_time": 1610000000
    }
  ],
  "tpf_name": "Shopee 3PF",
  "tpf_tracking_status": "3pf_warehouse_outbound_done"
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

## v2.logistics.batch_ship_order

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/batch_ship_order`

Use this api to batch initiate logistics including arrange pickup, dropoff or shipment for non-integrated logistic channels. Should call v2.logistics.get_shipping_parameter to fetch all required param first. It's recommended to initiate logistics one hour after the orders were placed since there is one-hour window buyer can cancel any order without request to seller.Only channel 90003 - Padrão in Brazil has the permission of this API.



### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/batch_ship_order` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/batch_ship_order` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/batch_ship_order` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/batch_ship_order` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of order. |
| ↳ order_sn | string | ✅ | 201212DCXHJUIKJ | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. Limit 150. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is't a package number. |
| pickup | object |  |  | Required parameter ONLY if GetParameterForInit returns "pickup" or if GetLogisticsInfo returns "pickup" under "info_needed" for the same order. Developer should still include "pickup" field in the call even if "pickup" has empty value.	 |
| ↳ address_id | int |  |  | The identity of address. Retrieved from shopee.logistics.GetAddress.	 |
| ↳ pickup_time_id | string |  |  | The pickup time id. Retrieved from shopee.logistics.GetTimeSlot.	 |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  |  | Need input this field when "tracking_number" is returned from "info_need". Please note that this tracking number is assigned by third-party shipping carrier for item shipment.	 |
| dropoff | object |  |  | Required parameter ONLY if GetParameterForInit returns "dropoff" or if GetLogisticsInfo returns "dropoff" under "info_needed" for the same order. Developer should still include "dropoff" field in the call even if "dropoff" has empty value. For logistic_id 80003 and 80004, both Regular and JOB shipping methods are supported. If you choose Regular shipping method, please use "tracking_no" to call Init API. If you choose JOB shipping method, please use "sender_real_name" to call Init API. Note that only one of "tracking_no" and "sender_real_name" can be selected. |
| ↳ branch_id | int |  | 0 | The identity of branch. Retrieved from shopee.logistics.GetBranch branch.	 |
| ↳ sender_real_name | string |  |  | The real name of sender.	 |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  |  | Need input this field when "tracking_number" is returned from "info_need". Please note that this tracking number is assigned by third-party shipping carrier for item shipment.	 |
| non_integrated | object |  |  | Optional parameter when GetParameterForInit returns "non-integrated" or GetLogisticsInfo returns "non-integrated" under "info_needed".	 |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  |  | Optional parameter for non-integrated channel order. The tracking number assigned by the shipping carrier for item shipment.	 |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 3dad66f43b8447d282ae6da36626c6b7 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | object[] |  |  | Indicate warning message you should take care. |
| ↳ order_sn | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. You should't fill the field with empty string when there is't a package number. |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ result_list | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ order_sn | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ ↳ package_number | string |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order.	 |
| ↳ ↳ fail_error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if one element hit error.	 |
| ↳ ↳ fail_message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if one element hit error.	 |

```json
        "result_list": [
            {
                "order_sn": "201212DCXHJUIKJ"
            },
            {
                "order_sn": "201212CXHQWEIKD"
            }
        ]

}
```

```json
    "error":"error_param",

    "request_id":"64c3649398f6c450fcd1a1f3edb8db78"
}
```

## v2.logistics.update_tracking_status

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/update_tracking_status`

Only available for Brazil sellers. This API is only available for orders/parcels which are fulfilled by BR Seller Logistics channel (logistics_channel_id: 90021), Samsung (logistics_channel_id: 90025) and BR Instant Delivery channel (logistics_channel_id: 90026). The logistics_status will become LOGISTICS_REQUEST_CREATED after arrange shipment, and can call this API to update to: LOGISTICS_PICKUP_DONE, LOGISTICS_DELIVERY_DONE, LOGISTICS_DELIVERY_FAILED.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_tracking_status` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/update_tracking_status` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/update_tracking_status` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_tracking_status` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| order_sn | string | ✅ | 201212DCXHJUIKJ | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| tracking_number | string |  | 1234567890 | Order tracking number, might help seller to identify his order on the tracking_URL.Can only be sent when updating logistics_status to "logistic_pickup_done". |
| tracking_url | string |  | https://tracking_url_order_201212DCXHJUIKJ | Website's URL for order tracking&nbsp;with maximum length of 2048 characters.Can only be sent when updating logistics_status to "logistic_pickup_done". |
| logistics_status | string | ✅ | logistics_pickup_done | Order status update support:- logistics_pickup_done- logistics_delivery_done- logistics_delivery_failed |
| failed_reason | string |  | buyer_unreachable | Only required when updating logistics_status to "logistics_delivery_failed". Only required for BR Instant Delivery channel (logistics_channel_id: 90026). Only accept the following values.&nbsp;-&nbsp;buyer_unreachable-&nbsp;buyer_unresponsive-&nbsp;no_delivery_location_consensus |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | e9b086380a7dcdc408cb5ebe2cf38041 | API request identifier |
| error | string |  |   | Error code |
| message | string |  |   | Error message |
| warning | string |  |   |  |
| response | object |  |  | Response body |

## v2.logistics.get_booking_shipping_parameter

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_parameter`

Use this api to get the parameter "info_needed" from the response to check if the booking has pickup or dropoff. This api will also return the addresses and pickup time id options for the pickup method. For dropoff, it can return branch id, sender real name etc, depending on the 3PL requirements.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_parameter` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_parameter` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_parameter` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_parameter` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| booking_sn | string | ✅ | 201214JASXYXY6 | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 2880a5a28510424eaa3288fd941fae2c | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. |  |
| response | object |  |  | Detail information you are querying. |
| ↳ info_needed | object |  |  | The parameters required based on each specific booking to Init. Must use the fields included under info_needed to call Init. |
| ↳ ↳ dropoff | string[] |  | [ ] | Could contain 'branch_id', 'sender_real_name' or 'tracking_no'. If it contains 'branch_id', choose one to Init. If it contains 'sender_real_name' or 'tracking_no', should manually input these values in Init API. If it has empty value, developer should still include "dropoff" field in Init API. |
| ↳ ↳ pickup | string[] |  | ["address_id", "pickup_time_id"] | Could contain 'address_id' and 'pickup_time_id'. Choose one address_id and its corresponding pickup_time_id to Init. If it has empty value, developer should still include "pickup" field in Init API.It could contains "tracking_number" returned from "info_need"for some channels, please also add it when init. |
| ↳ pickup | object |  |  | Logistics information for pickup mode booking. |
| ↳ ↳ address_list | object[] |  |  | List of available pickup address info. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address_id | int64 |  | 234 | The identity of address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ region | string |  | SG | The region of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ state | string |  | Sarawak | The state of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ city | string |  |  | The city of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ district | string |  |  | The district of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ town | string |  | haha | The town of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address | string |  |  | The address description of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ zipcode | string |  | 50003 | The zipcode of specify address. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ address_flag | string[] |  |  | The flag of shop address, applicable values: default_address, pickup_address, return_address, current_address(only for multi-warehouse sellers) |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ time_slot_list | object[] |  |  | List of pickup_time information corresponding to the address_id.Some logistics channels may not return any date or time for pickup time slots. In such cases, sellers can arrange shipment without selecting any time slot, and Shopee will arrange a suitable timing for these situations. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ date | timestamp |  |  | The date of pickup time. In timestamp. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ time_text | string |  |  | The text description of pickup time. Only applicable for certain channels. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ pickup_time_id | string |  |  | The identity of pickuptime. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ flags | string[] |  | ["recommended"] | This field will have the value&nbsp;“recommended”&nbsp;for the time slot that Shopee suggests sellers choose.&nbsp;While it is advisable for sellers to choose the recommended time slot, they can also choose other time slots that do not have the recommended flag. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ error | string |  |  | return if error getting pickup time, otherwise omitted |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ msg | string |  |  | return if error getting pickup time, otherwise omitted |

```python
}
```

## v2.logistics.ship_booking

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/ship_booking`

Use this api to initiate logistics including arrange pickup, dropoff. Should call v2.logistics.get_booking_shipping_parameter to fetch all required param first.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/ship_booking` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/ship_booking` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/ship_booking` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/ship_booking` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| booking_sn | string | ✅ | 201214JASXYXY6 | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |
| pickup | object |  |  | Required parameter ONLY if get_shipping_parameter returns "pickup" under "info_needed". Developer should still include "pickup" field in the call even if "pickup" has empty value. |
| ↳ address_id | int64 | ✅ |  | The identity of address. Retrieved from v2.logistics.get_booking_shipping_parameter. |
| ↳ pickup_time_id | string |  |  | The pickup time id. Retrieved from v2.logistics.get_shipping_booking_parameter, you can only select one from the time_slot_list.Some logistics channels may not return any date or time for pickup time slots. In such cases, sellers can arrange shipment without selecting any time slot, and Shopee will arrange a suitable timing for these situations. |
| dropoff | object |  |  | Required parameter ONLY if get_shipping_parameter returns "dropoff" under "info_needed". Developer should still include "dropoff" field in the call even if "dropoff" has empty value. If you choose Regular shipping method, please use "tracking_no" to call Init API. If you choose JOB shipping method, please use "sender_real_name" to call Init API. Note that only one of "tracking_no" and "sender_real_name" can be selected. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 2880a5a28510424eaa3288fd941fae2c | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://open.admin.uat.shopee.io/api/v2/logistics/ship_booking?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "booking_sn": "201214JASXYXY6",
  "dropoff": {},
  "pickup": {
    "pickup_time_id": "-"
  }
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```json
	"booking_sn": "201214JASXYXY6",
	"pickup": {
		"pickup_time_id": "-"
	},
	"dropoff": {}
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
	"request_id": "2880a5a28510424eaa3288fd941fae2c",
	"error": "error_auth",
	"message": "Invalid access_token."
}
```

### Error Example

```json
   "error": "common.unknown_error",
   "message": "Unknown error, detail: error_key:FULFILLMENT_BOOKING_DOES_NOT_EXIST.",
   "request_id": "635c3210a2cf49ab97c64226e6edb531"
}
```

## v2.logistics.get_booking_tracking_number

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_tracking_number`

After arranging shipment (v2.logistics.ship_booking) for the integrated channel, use this api to get the tracking_number, which is a required parameter for creating shipping labels. The api response can return tracking_number empty, since this info is dependent from the 3PL, due to this it is allowed to keep calling the api within 5 minutes interval, until the tracking_number is returned.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_tracking_number` |

| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |

## v2.logistics.get_booking_shipping_document_parameter

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_parameter`

Use this api to get the selectable shipping_document_type and suggested shipping_document_type.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_parameter` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_parameter` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_parameter` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| booking_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of bookings you want to get. limit [1,50] |
| ↳ booking_sn | string | ✅ | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | a8e1b94f51d64540bf5762abe7783073 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | common.error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | object[] |  |  | Indicate warning message you should take care. |
| ↳ booking_sn | string |  | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ result_list | object[] |  |  | The list of the result data. |
| ↳ ↳ booking_sn | string |  | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |
| ↳ ↳ suggest_shipping_document_type | string |  | THERMAL_AIR_WAYBILL | The shipping document type Shopee suggests. If you don't select any shipping document type, Shopee will use this as default shipping document type. |
| ↳ ↳ selectable_shipping_document_type | string[] |  | ["THERMAL_AIR_WAYBILL"] | The shipping document type you can select of this booking. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_error | string |  | logistics.booking_not_exist | Indicate error type if one element hit error. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_message | string |  | The booking_sn 201201V81SYYDG you provided is not exist.  | Indicate error details if one element hit error. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://open.admin.uat.shopee.io/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_parameter?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "booking_list": [
    {
      "booking_sn": "201201E81SYYKE"
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```json
	"booking_list": [
		{
			"booking_sn": "201201E81SYYKE"
		}
	]
}
```

```json
	"request_id": "a8e1b94f51d64540bf5762abe7783073",
	"error": "common.error_auth",
	"message": "Invalid access_token.",
	"warning": [
		{
			"booking_sn": "201201E81SYYKE"
		}
	],
	"response": {
		"result_list": [
			{
				"booking_sn": "201201E81SYYKE",
				"suggest_shipping_document_type": "THERMAL_AIR_WAYBILL",
				"selectable_shipping_document_type": [
					"THERMAL_AIR_WAYBILL"
				],
				"fail_error": "logistics.booking_not_exist",
				"fail_message": "The booking_sn 201201V81SYYDG you provided is not exist. "
			}
		]
	}
```

## v2.logistics.create_booking_shipping_document

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/create_booking_shipping_document`

Use this api to create shipping document task for each booking and this API is only available after retrieving the tracking number.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/create_booking_shipping_document` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/create_booking_shipping_document` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/create_booking_shipping_document` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/create_booking_shipping_document` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| booking_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of bookings you want to get. limit [1,50] |
| ↳ booking_sn | string | ✅ | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |
| ↳ tracking_number | string |  | SPXID02742637123B | The tracking number of booking. Required except for the channel allow print before arrange shipment. |
| ↳ shipping_document_type | string |  | NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL | The type of shipping document. Available values: NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL,THERMAL_AIR_WAYBILL,NORMAL_JOB_AIR_WAYBILL,THERMAL_JOB_AIR_WAYBILL |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | a8e1b94f51d64540bf5762abe7783073 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | common.error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | object[] |  |  | Indicate warning message you should take care. |
| ↳ booking_sn | string |  | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ result_list | object[] |  |  | The list of the result data. |
| ↳ ↳ booking_sn | string |  | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_error | string |  | logistics.booking_not_exist | Indicate error type if one element hit error. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_message | string |  | The booking_sn 201201V81SYYDG you provided is not exist.  | Indicate error details if one element hit error. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://open.admin.uat.shopee.io/api/v2/logistics/create_booking_shipping_document?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "booking_list": [
    {
      "booking_sn": "201201E81SYYKE",
      "shipping_document_type": "NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL",
      "tracking_number": "SPXID02742637123B"
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```json
		}
	]
}
```

```json
			}
		]
	}
}
```
