## v2.logistics.download_booking_shipping_document

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/download_booking_shipping_document`

Use this api to download shipping_document. You have to call v2.logistics.create_booking_shipping_document to create a new shipping document task first and call v2.logistics.get_booking_shipping_document_result to get the task status second. If the task is READY, you can download this shipping document.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/download_booking_shipping_document` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/download_booking_shipping_document` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/download_booking_shipping_document` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/download_booking_shipping_document` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| shipping_document_type | string |  | NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL | The type of shipping document. Available values: NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL,THERMAL_AIR_WAYBILL |
| booking_list | object[] | ✅ |  | The list of bookings you want to get. limit [1,50] |
| ↳ booking_sn | string | ✅ | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| waybill | file |  |  | The waybill file. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://open.admin.uat.shopee.io/api/v2/logistics/download_booking_shipping_document?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

  "booking_list": [
    {
      "booking_sn": "201201E81SYYKE"
    }
  ],
  "shipping_document_type": "NORMAL_AIR_WAYBILL"
})
headers = {

print(response.text)
```

```json
		}
	]
}
```

## v2.logistics.get_booking_shipping_document_data_info

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_data_info`

Use this api to fetch the logistics information of a booking these info can be used for airwaybill printing. Dedicated for crossborder SLS order airwaybill. May not be applicable for local channel airwaybill. Besides, this api supports returning personal info as images.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_data_info` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_data_info` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_data_info` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_data_info` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| booking_sn | string | ✅ | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |
| recipient_address_info | object[] |  |  | recipient address to query as image |
| ↳ key | string | ✅ | name | fields to query in the recipient address, should be&nbsp;name,&nbsp;phone,&nbsp;full_address,&nbsp;town,&nbsp;district,&nbsp;city,&nbsp;state,&nbsp;region,&nbsp;zipcode. |
| ↳ style | object |  |  | image style |
| ↳ ↳ text_style | string[] |  | ["bold", "italic"] | supports bold and italic |
| ↳ ↳ font_size | int64 |  | 12 | supports 1 - 108 |
| ↳ ↳ text_color | string |  | #39c5bb | color string like "#AbCd12" |
| ↳ ↳ image_width | float |  | 3.0 | supports 0.1-30, in centimeters |
| ↳ ↳ h_align | string |  | center | text horizontal align, supports left, center and right. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 862fc4d77de74edf9bf045c7650f1988 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ recipient_address_info | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ key | string |  | name | queried field in recipient address |
| ↳ ↳ image | string |  | data:image/png;base64,/9j/4AAQSkZJRgABAgEASABIAAD/…… | base64 encoded png data string |
| ↳ shipping_document_info | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ booking_weight | int64 |  | 500 | Use this field to indicate booking weight when calculate the shipping fee. The unit of weigh is gram. |
| ↳ ↳ logistics_channel_id | int64 |  | 28016 | The identity of logistic channel. |
| ↳ ↳ shipping_carrier | string |  | Standard Delivery | The logistics service provider for the booking. |
| ↳ ↳ recipient_sort_code | object |  |  | The sort_code of recipient. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ first_recipient_sort_code | string |  | B-151-ATP-ATP-010 | The first-level sort_code of recipient. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ second_recipient_sort_code | string |  |  | The second-level sort_code of recipient. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ third_recipient_sort_code | string |  | SAN ROQUE 009 | The third-level sort_code of recipient. |
| ↳ ↳ sender_sort_code | object |  |  | The sort_code of sender. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ first_sender_sort_code | string |  |  | The first-level sort_code of sender. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ second_sender_sort_code | string |  |  | The second-level sort_code of sender. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ third_sender_sort_code | string |  |  | The third-level sort_code of sender. |
| ↳ ↳ return_sort_code | object |  |  | The sort code for 3PL doing RTS. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ return_first_sort_code | string |  | RTS-FM-MAN | The first-level sort code for 3PL doing RTS. |
| ↳ ↳ tracking_number | string |  | MY200448706479IT | The tracking number assigned by the shipping carrier for item shipment. |
| ↳ ↳ pickup_hub | string |  |  | The name of pickup hub. |
| ↳ ↳ delivery_hub | string |  |  | The name of delivery hub. |
| ↳ ↳ deliver_area | string |  | SAN ROQUE 009 | Zone name. |
| ↳ ↳ ec_booking_no | string |  |  | The name of ec booing. |
| ↳ ↳ create_date_ymd_sl | string |  | 2022/12/05 | The date of create shipment booking. |
| ↳ ↳ manufacturers_name | string |  |  | The name of manufacturer. |
| ↳ ↳ manufacturers_website | string |  |  | The website of manufacturer. |
| ↳ ↳ is_lm_dg_bool | int64 |  | 0 | Use this field to indicate booking contains dangerous goods or not. dg:1 non-dg:0 |
| ↳ ↳ spx_sub_district | string |  |  | The sub-district of recipient's address. |
| ↳ ↳ spx_receive_station | object |  |  | The spx receive station. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ spx_first_receive_station | string |  |  | The first pickup station. |
| ↳ ↳ zone | string |  |  | The zone of this booking. |
| ↳ ↳ zone_code | string |  |  | Delivery Sub Zone. |
| ↳ ↳ destination_base_code | string |  |  | Distribution Center Code. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://open.admin.uat.shopee.io/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_shipping_document_data_info?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "booking_sn": "201201E81SYYKE",
  "recipient_address_info": [
    {
      "key": "name",
      "style": {
        "h_align": "center",
        "image_width": 3,
        "text_color": "#39c5bb",
        "text_style": [
          "bold",
          "italic"
        ]
      }
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"booking_sn": "201201E81SYYKE",
	"recipient_address_info": [
		{
			"key": "name",
			"style": {
				"text_style": [
					"bold",
					"italic"
				],
				"text_color": "#39c5bb",
				"image_width": 3,
				"h_align": "center"
			}
		}
	]
}
```

```json
				"return_first_sort_code": "RTS-FM-MAN"
```

## v2.logistics.get_booking_tracking_info

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_tracking_info`

Use this api to get the logistics tracking information of a booking.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_tracking_info` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_tracking_info` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_tracking_info` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_tracking_info` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| booking_sn | string | ✅ | 201201E81SYYKE | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 862fc4d77de74edf9bf045c7650f1988 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ booking_sn | string |  | 201214JASXYXY6 | Shopee's unique identifier for a booking. |
| ↳ logistics_status | string |  | LOGISTICS_REQUEST_CREATED | The Shopee logistics status for the booking. Applicable values.LOGISTICS_REQUEST_CREATED:booking arranged shipmentLOGISTICS_PICKUP_DONE:booking handed over to 3PLLOGISTICS_PICKUP_FAILED:booking&nbsp;cancelled by 3PL due to failed pickup or picked up but not able to proceed with deliveryLOGISTICS_DELIVERY_DONE:successfully deliveredLOGISTICS_REQUEST_CANCELED:cancelled when booking at LOGISTICS_REQUEST_CREATEDLOGISTICS_READY:booking&nbsp;ready for fulfilmentLOGISTICS_INVALID:cancelled when booking at LOGISTICS_READYLOGISTICS_LOST:booking cancelled due to 3PL lost the parcel |
| ↳ tracking_info | object[] |  |  | The tracking info of the booking. |
| ↳ ↳ update_time | timestamp |  | 1587375600 | The time when logistics info has been updated. |
| ↳ ↳ description | string |  | Your parcel is ready to ship from the seller | The description of booking logistics tracking info.logistics_status |
| ↳ ↳ logistics_status | string |  | PICKED_UP | The Shopee logistics status for the booking.&nbsp;TrackingLogisticsStatus:INITIALORDER_INITORDER_SUBMITTEDORDER_CREATEDPICKUP_REQUESTEDPICKUP_PENDINGPICKED_UPDELIVERY_PENDINGDELIVEREDLOSTUPDATEUPDATE_SUBMITTEDUPDATE_CREATEDRETURN_STARTEDRETURN_PENDINGCANCELCANCEL_CREATEDCANCELEDFAILED_ORDER_SUBMITTEDFAILED_ORDER_CREATEDFAILED_PICKUP_REQUESTEDFAILED_PICKED_UPFAILED_DELIVEREDFAILED_UPDATE_SUBMITTEDFAILED_UPDATE_CREATEDFAILED_RETURNEDFAILED_CANCEL_CREATEDFAILED_CANCELEDRETURNEDRETURN_INTIATED |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.uat.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_booking_tracking_info?access_token=access_token&booking_sn=201201E81SYYKE&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.RPCRequest("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```


```json
	"request_id": "862fc4d77de74edf9bf045c7650f1988",
```

## v2.logistics.download_to_label

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/download_to_label`

Use the API to download the TO label that should be attached to the carton before drop-off at the warehouse (Only for TW channel_id:30029).

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/download_to_label` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/download_to_label` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/download_to_label` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/download_to_label` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| sorting_group | int32 | ✅ | 1 | Sorting Group of the TO. Available value:1:North2:South |
| quantity | int32 |  | 1 | Specifies the TO quantity, up to a maximum of 20 per request. If not specified, the default value is 1 |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| waybill | file |  |  | The waybill file. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/download_to_label?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "quantity": 1,
  "sorting_group": 1
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"sorting_group": 1,
	"quantity": 1
}
```

### Response Example

```json

	"waybill": "path/to/file"
}
```

### Error Example

```json

    "error": "logistics.can_not_print_to_label",
    "message": "You do not have permission to call this API because you are either not enabled for channel 30029 or do not have any ongoing orders in this channel.",
    "request_id": "e3e3e7f33875f1ed0d07620d9042a201:010003d1c0bc23d3:0000001748f5a448"
}
```

## v2.logistics.create_shipping_document_job

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document_job`

This API creates a shipping document job for selected documents. The system receives requests and returns a job ID along with success and failure details.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document_job` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document_job` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document_job` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document_job` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| shipping_document_type | string | ✅ | THERMAL_UNPACKAGED_LABEL | The type of shipping document. Available values: THERMAL_UNPACKAGED_LABEL |
| unpackaged_sku_requests | object[] |  |  | List of Unpackaged SKUs to generate labels for.Note: The unpackaged_sku_requests and package_list cannot be populated at the same time, please select one. |
| ↳ unpackaged_sku_id | string |  | 844091087_11192347 | Unpackaged SKU ID of the model. |
| ↳ quantity | int64 |  | 10 | Number of copies for the generated labels (maximum 600 total across all requested SKUs). |
| package_list | string[] |  |  | List of Package Numbers to generate labels for.&nbsp;(maximum 600 total)Note: The unpackaged_sku_requests and package_list cannot be populated at the same time, please select one. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 5551ce8db5314c70a362dfe33544f074 | The identifier of the API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ job_id | string |  | SDK0001_a86148a97a6e04ce2ed468968de344b7 | Generated Job ID which will be used for status tracking and download the Shipping Document |
| ↳ success_id_list | string[] |  | ["844091087_11192347"] | List of Package Number or Unpackaged SKU ID that succeeds in generating Shipping Document |
| ↳ fail_list | object[] |  |  | List of Package Numbers or Unpackaged SKUs that failed in generating Shipping Document |
| ↳ ↳ id | string |  | 844091087_11192342 | Package Number or Unpackaged SKU ID that failed in generating Shipping Document |
| ↳ ↳ fail_error | string |  | error_param | Indicate error type if one element hit error. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_message | string |  | Wrong parameters, detail: This Unpackaged SKU ID is not eligible for printing. Reason: Invalid SKU ID. | Indicate error details if one element hit error. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/create_shipping_document_job?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "shipping_document_type": "THERMAL_UNPACKAGED_LABEL",
  "unpackaged_sku_requests": [
    {
      "quantity": 10,
      "unpackaged_sku_id": "844091087_11192347"
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"shipping_document_type": "THERMAL_UNPACKAGED_LABEL",
	"unpackaged_sku_requests": [
		{
			"unpackaged_sku_id": "844091087_11192347",
			"quantity": 10
		}
	]
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
	"request_id": "5551ce8db5314c70a362dfe33544f074",
	"error": "",
	"message": "",
	"response": {
		"job_id": "SDK0001_a86148a97a6e04ce2ed468968de344b7",
		"success_id_list": [
			"844091087_11192347"
		],
		"fail_list": []
	}
}
```

| --- | --- |

| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |

## v2.logistics.get_shipping_document_job_status

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_job_status`

This API retrieves the status of a shipping document job using the job ID provided.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_job_status` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_job_status` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_job_status` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_job_status` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| job_id | string | ✅ | SDK0001_a86148a97a6e04ce2ed468968de344b7 | Generated Job ID for status tracking and download the Shipping Document |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 5551ce8db5314c70a362dfe33544f074 | The identifier of the API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |

```python
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/get_shipping_document_job_status?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

  "job_id": "SDK0001_a86148a97a6e04ce2ed468968de344b7"
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```python
import requests
import json
```

## v2.logistics.update_self_collection_order_logistics

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/update_self_collection_order_logistics`

Use this api to update the order status for buyer to collect the orders directly from your pharmacy. This includes indicating that order is ready for collection, and that the order has been picked up by the buyer. You should call v2.logistics.get_order_detail or v2.logistics.get_package_detail first to get the package_number of such orders.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_self_collection_order_logistics` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/update_self_collection_order_logistics` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/update_self_collection_order_logistics` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_self_collection_order_logistics` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| package_number | string | ✅ | OFG211171863281841 | Shopee's unique identifier for the package under an order. |
| self_collection_logistics_action | string | ✅ | order_collected | Order logistics action. available values:- ready_for_collection- order_collected |
| epoc_image_list | string[] |  | ["id-11134284-7r98o-mef6xcoiw1nt15"] | List of image_id for the proof that buyer already collected the order at the store.&nbsp;Required when self_collection_logistics_action is order_collected. Max: 3.You can call the v2.media.upload_image to upload image and get the image_id, for this scenario, please pass the business = 1 and scene = 1. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  | error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| request_id | string |  | 3dad66f43b8447d282ae6da36626c6b7 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/update_self_collection_order_logistics?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "epoc_image_list": [
    "id-11134284-7r98o-mef6xcoiw1nt15"
  ],
  "package_number": "OFG211171863281841",
  "self_collection_logistics_action": "order_collected"
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```json
	"package_number": "OFG211171863281841",
	"self_collection_logistics_action": "order_collected",

		"id-11134284-7r98o-mef6xcoiw1nt15"
	]
}
```

```json
	"error": "logistics.not_available_for_self_collection",

}
```

```json
}
```

## v2.logistics.set_mart_packaging_info

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/set_mart_packaging_info`

[Only for ID mart seller] This API allows sellers to set up their packaging fee info. Through this API, sellers can enable or disable packaging fees, and if enabled, specify the dimensions of the packaging and the associated fee. This ensures that sellers can configure their shipping costs accurately based on their packaging requirements.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/set_mart_packaging_info` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/set_mart_packaging_info` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/set_mart_packaging_info` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/set_mart_packaging_info` |

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| enable | boolean | ✅ | true | Indicates whether the seller has enabled or disabled the packaging fee configuration.True:&nbsp;The seller charges a packaging fee.False:&nbsp;The seller does not charge a packaging fee. |
| dimension | object |  |  | Required if&nbsp;enabled&nbsp;is set to&nbsp;True. |
| ↳ length | int32 | ✅ | 15 | The length of the packaging in centimetres (cm). |
| ↳ width | int32 | ✅ | 15 | The width of the packaging in centimetres (cm). |
| ↳ height | int32 | ✅ | 15 | The height of the packaging in centimetres (cm). |
| packaging_fee | object |  |  | Required if&nbsp;enabled&nbsp;is set to&nbsp;True. |
| ↳ value | float | ✅ | 100.00 | The packaging fee price in your region's local currency.For SG/MY/BR/MX seller: Sellers can set the price with two decimal place, other regions can only set the price as an integer. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| ↳ dimension | object |  |  | Returned only if&nbsp;enabled&nbsp;is set to&nbsp;True. |
| ↳ ↳ length | int32 |  | 15 | The length of the packaging in centimetres (cm). |
| ↳ ↳ width | int32 |  | 15 | The width of the packaging in centimetres (cm). |
| ↳ ↳ height | int32 |  | 15 | The height of the packaging in centimetres (cm). |
| ↳ packaging_fee | object |  |  | Returned only if&nbsp;enabled&nbsp;is set to&nbsp;True. |
| ↳ ↳ value | float |  | 100.00 | The packaging fee price in the seller's local currency. |

```python
import json

print(response.text)
```

## v2.logistics.upload_serviceable_polygon

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/logistics/upload_serviceable_polygon`

Only available for Brazil sellers. Use this API to upload KML file for shop level serviceability setting for BR Entrega Turbo channel (Channel ID: 90026). Please note that multiple Outlet Shops under the same Mart Shop cannot have overlapping service areas.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/upload_serviceable_polygon` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/logistics/upload_serviceable_polygon` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/logistics/upload_serviceable_polygon` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/upload_serviceable_polygon` |

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| file | file | ✅ |  | The .kml file to be uploaded to denote the serviceability area of the shops.Note: Please refer to&nbsp;“KML file format for v2.logistics.upload_serviceable_polygon”&nbsp;to understand the structure specifications and upload requirements for KML files. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  | error_param | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | File size exceeds max allowable size. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| request_id | string |  | a316752d608baf000b2c73e2e5eed5c8 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ task_id | string |  |  | Use the task_id to call v2.logistics.check_polygon_update_status to check if the upload job has been completed. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/upload_polygon?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
files=[
  ('file',('file',open('/path/to/file','rb'),'application/octet-stream')) # Replace with actual file path
]
headers = {

}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, files=files, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```json
	"file": "path/to/file"
}
```

```json
	"error": "",
	"message": "",
	"request_id": "a316752d608baf000b2c73e2e5eed5c8",

	}
```

| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| task_id | string | ✅ | test_task_id | ID that needs to be checked. Please pass the task_id returned via the v2.logistics.upload_serviceable_polygon. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  | error_server | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Error Details : task not found | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| request_id | string |  | a316752d608baf000b2c73e2e5eed5c8 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ status | int32 |  | 0 | Serviceable polygon file upload status. Applicable values:&nbsp;0: Task completed1: Task in progress2: KML file related errors |
| ↳ message | string |  | task completed | Details of the upload status, e.g "task in progress". |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/logistics/check_polygon_update_status?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "task_id": "test_task_id"
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"task_id": "test_task_id"
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
	"error": "",
	"message": "",
        "request_id": "a316752d608baf000b2c73e2e5eed5c8",
	"response": {
		"status": 1,
		"message": "task in progress"
	}
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
	"error": "",
	"message": "",
        "request_id": "a316752d608baf000b2c73e2e5eed5c8"
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `logistics.invalid_address_version` | Some addresses are no longer supported per new regulation, please update to avoid wrong fulfilment |
| `error_server` | Error Details : {msg} |
